# Doppler Electron Design Partner Pipeline

- contract: valid
- researched prospects: 5
- primary wave: 3
- qualified customers represented here: 0

This register is a researched outreach pipeline. A listed application is not a customer, design partner, authorized workload, qualified integration, production release, revenue source, or evidence of demand until separately retained customer authority and qualification evidence establish that state.

## Ordered targets

1. **AnythingLLM** — primary; research-candidate
   Pilot: Qualify one planned local-model release and its subsequent upgrade across the customer-declared AnythingLLM desktop provider and hardware matrix.
   Open gates: outreach-not-authorized, customer-relationship-unestablished, application-revision-not-pinned, acceptance-suite-not-authorized, paid-production-release-unestablished, customer-fleet-receipts-missing, subsequent-upgrade-unestablished
2. **Joplin** — primary; research-candidate
   Pilot: Turn one planned Joplin semantic-search model release into an externally maintained embedding Capsule with index-compatible upgrade and rollback.
   Open gates: outreach-not-authorized, customer-relationship-unestablished, application-revision-not-pinned, acceptance-suite-not-authorized, paid-production-release-unestablished, customer-fleet-receipts-missing, subsequent-upgrade-unestablished
3. **Cherry Studio** — primary; research-candidate
   Pilot: Qualify one local generation release, one embedding release, and their provider metadata for a customer-declared Cherry Studio desktop matrix.
   Open gates: outreach-not-authorized, customer-relationship-unestablished, application-revision-not-pinned, acceptance-suite-not-authorized, paid-production-release-unestablished, customer-fleet-receipts-missing, subsequent-upgrade-unestablished
4. **Chatbox** — backup; research-candidate
   Pilot: Qualify one knowledge-base release with authentic embedding, reranking, and provider declarations in the Chatbox desktop application.
   Open gates: outreach-not-authorized, customer-relationship-unestablished, application-revision-not-pinned, acceptance-suite-not-authorized, paid-production-release-unestablished, customer-fleet-receipts-missing, subsequent-upgrade-unestablished
5. **AFFiNE** — later; research-candidate
   Pilot: Hold AFFiNE as a later document-intelligence prospect until a consequential local-model release and its application acceptance boundary are confirmed by the application owner.
   Open gates: outreach-not-authorized, consequential-local-model-release-not-confirmed, customer-relationship-unestablished, application-revision-not-pinned, acceptance-suite-not-authorized, paid-production-release-unestablished, customer-fleet-receipts-missing, subsequent-upgrade-unestablished
